# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/careers/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/partners/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# WE BUILD LASTING & IMPACTFUL   PARTNERSHIPSP

Going Beyond Borders: Impacting Change

With our strategic position and extensive network in Southeast Asia, we benefit from a continual and scalable supply of agricultural materials from farmer cooperatives and major producers that accelerate our access to these resources.

Going Beyond Borders: Impacting Change

With our strategic position and extensive network in Southeast Asia, we benefit from a continual and scalable supply of agricultural materials from farmer cooperatives and major producers that accelerate our access to these resources.

### In partnership with farmer cooperatives in Southeast Asia and into 2024, we are expanding further across the region, with a particular focus on the growth in The Philippines.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/palf-features/chemicals/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# GOING BEYOND: ENVISIONING TOMORROW’S FUTURE, TODAY

## CIRCULARITY

Towards a Waste-Free World with Nextevo

Envisioning a waste-free world starts with a circular economy, based on the principles of designing out waste and pollution, keeping materials in use, and regenerating our natural environment.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/beginnings/future-innovation/)

# Our Unwavering Commitment to the Environment

## What Drives Us

![Nextevo Driving US Mission Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/IMG_7266.png)

OUR VISION

Empower sustainability through innovation and best practices by transforming natural fibers at scale into textile and fashion solutions.

OUR MISSION

Deliver high-quality,

sustainable textiles to over 100 million consumers by 2030.

![Nextevo Driving US Vision Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/Mask-Group-41.png)

## Our Three Principle Pillars

![Three Principle Elevating Lives Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Elevating_Lives.png)

Elevating Lives

Supplement incomes of farmer communities through agricultural residue/waste collection and processing

![Three Principle Preserving the Planet Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Preserving_the_Planet.png)

Preserving the Planet

Reduce massive amounts of carbon and water footprints, and preserve our planet’s beauty.

![Three Principle Innovate & Diversify Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/InnovateDiversify.png)

Innovate & Diversify

Explore new materials to ensure circularity and drive sustainable evolution in the global textile and fashion industry.

## Our Journey Since 2019

4TH QUARTER

2ND QUARTER

2024

2ND QUARTER

Overcoming Obstacles and Scale-up Plan: Nextevo to establish its first owned ready-to-spin (RTS) fibre facility in Southeast Asia

# EXPLORE OUR SUSTAINABILITY-DRIVEN INITIATIVES

![Explore Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/IMG_8183-2-Edited.png)

Driven by innovation to cultivate a positive social and environmental legacy.

Our sustainability-led approaches are the intersection in which Planet, People and Partnerships converge. We aim to inspire a future where excellence meets environmental stewardship.

Our

Initiatives


![image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Farming-Connection-Supplement-Farmers-Income-1.png)![image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/04/ZeroWaste.png)![image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Mask-Group-6-1.png)![image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Farming-Connection_-Improve-Soil-Conditions-1.png)![image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/Farming-Connection_Fight-Climate-Change-1.png)

## Fostering Social Impact

![Fostering Social Impact](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/IMG_7266.png)![Fostering Social Impact](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/OUR-INITIATIVES_mobile3.png)

Ground-up initiatives like creation of employment opportunities, education and skill enhancements are just some of the few that demonstrates our support towards the smallholder farming communities in Southeast Asia.

![Fostering Social Impact](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/IMG_6665.png)![Fostering Social Impact](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/OUR-INITIATIVES_mobile4.png)

Traditionally burnt or dumped, Nextevo now transforms agricultural byproducts (e.g. pineapple leaves) into sustainable value-added materials, benefiting various farmer groups in Southeast Asia at farm level.

## Zero Waste Philosophy

We are championing a zero waste philosophy, diligently maximizing the use of all agricultural byproducts and residues to valorize them into circular, value-added and purposeful materials.

One example, residues are what remain after PALF extraction. Nextevo is resolutely dedicated to repurposing these ‘new’ resources.

[Explore Nextevo’s\\
Solutions](https://nextevo.one/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

![Zero Waste Philosophy](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/IMG_6997-1.png)![Zero Waste Philosophy](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/OUR-INITIATIVES_mobile2.png)

## Aligning with UN SDGs

Aligned with the United Nations Sustainable Development Goals (UN SDGs), our efforts contribute to Goals 8 (Decent Work and Economic Growth), 9 (Industry, Innovation, and Infrastructure), 12 (Responsible Consumption and Production), 13 (Climate Action), and 17 (Partnerships for the Goals).

[Learn\\
More](https://sdgs.un.org/goals)

![Aligning with UN SDGs](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Our-Initiatives3.png)![Aligning](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/Our-Initiatives3-1.png)

## Traceable Supply Chain

At Nextevo, we meticulously craft a traceable supply chain, connecting the roots of our products at the farm level to our processing stages. Our commitment is reinforced by a certificate of origin, providing traceability back to our farms and dedicated farmer cooperatives in Southeast Asia.

Fostering transparency, trust and sustainability from cultivation to processing, join us in reshaping the textile narrative towards a sustainable future.

![Traceable Supply Chain](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1648724884.png)![Traceable Supply Chain](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/03/MobileInitiatives-4.png)

## Minimizing Environmental Footprints

![Minimizing](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2023/12/iStock-1483209675-Edited.png)![Minimizing Environmental Footprints](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/OUR-INITIATIVES_mobile5.png)

Southeast Asia is one of the world’s largest agricultural hubs; it is either the world’s largest or top three producers for pineapples, coconuts, rice, rubber, sugar cane, palm oil, cassava, etc. Significant volumes of agricultural wastes are commonly burnt and dumped, resulting in harmful carbon emissions.

![Minimizing Environmental Footprints](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/anne-nygard-yPJeQRCeOdk-unsplash-1.png)![Minimizing Environmental Footprints](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/OUR-INITIATIVES_mobile6.png)

At Nextevo, the value of sustainability is at the core of our production ethos. We transform agricultural byproducts and other natural fibers to craft innovative textile materials without imposing any additional burden on land, water and other resources.

Our ground-up initiatives generate additional income streams for farming communities in Southeast Asia. We’re set to expand our sourcing network further, amplifying our impact in the region.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/beginnings/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# WHERE FASHION MEETS SUSTAINABILITY

## What is PALF?

### Step into a future where sustainability meets innovation with our Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF).

Cultivate without any additional land, water or other resources. PALF emerges from pineapple leaf byproducts valorization, transforming what was once waste into eco-friendly textiles.

![Product Intro Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/palf-product.jpeg)

Unmanaged and excessive pineapple waste in the region amplifies methane pollution, harming both air quality and crop health.

Our PALF offers a combination of sustainability, products capability and scalability

## Our PALF Products

![Product Palf Fiber Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/photo_2024-01-12-07.56.10.jpeg)

Ready-To-Spin (RTS) Fiber

Reminiscent of cotton fibers, our RTS PALF are meticulously refined through a carding machine and transforming them into high-quality, smooth, natural white long staple fibers of high mechanical strength.

Produced on a commercial scale, our fibres are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, ensuring they meet the growing demand for safe and environmentally friendly materials. Notably, it exhibits significant antibacterial properties against staphylococcus Aureus DSM 799, ensuring skin-friendly properties.

[Learn More](https://nextevo.one/vietnam/)

PALF-Blended Yarn

Our blended yarns are a seamless fusion of eco-fibers, blending our RTS PALF with cotton, and lyocell and/or recycled polyester.

Created to harness the strength of PALF and secondary fibers, our blends achieve functionality, whether it’s through biodegradability or durability. These yarns are available in ring-spinning and open-end variations, ranging from Ne 8-30 counts.

![Product Palf Yarn Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/photo_2024-01-12-07.56.20-1.jpeg)

![Product Palf Fabric Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/PALF-Blended-Fabrics.png)

PALF-Blended Fabrics

From denim to woven and knits, these medium to heavyweight PALF-blended fabrics redefine elegance with every thread. Crafted from our yarns, versatility is our hallmark. Tailored for diverse applications across fashion and beyond.

Whether it be adorning haute couture or elevating everyday wear, our PALF-blended fabrics fuse eco-responsibility with texture and capabilities.

![Product Palf Fiber Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/photo_2024-01-12-07.56.10.jpeg)

### Ready-To-Spin (RTS) Fiber

Produced on a commercial scale, our fibres are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, ensuring they meet the growing demand for safe and environmentally friendly materials. Notably, it exhibits significant antibacterial properties against staphylococcus Aureus DSM 799, ensuring skin-friendly properties. [Learn More](https://nextevo.one/vietnam/)

## From Farm to Fibre Processing

With FibreTrace® ensuring farm-to-fibre traceability, our journey from farm to textiles highlights our dedication to sustainable practices. Versatile and adaptable, PALF is ideal for four key end applications: apparel and accessories, footwear, home textiles, and interior furnishings.

# INTRODUCING OUR Vietnam Pineapple Leaf Fibre Processing Facility

Our new facility in Dong Nai, Vietnam, boosts sustainable textile innovation as a strategic logistics hub near major seaports, reducing transportation costs and carbon emissions.

![Nextevo Vietnam Mission Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/Picture-1.png)

## What We Do

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.54-1.png)

### Initial Processing

After harvesting, the fibers are transported to our facility, where we begin by brushing (optional) them to remove impurities. We then cut the fibers to the desired length for further refinement.

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.55-1.png)

### Refinement

Next, the fibers undergo pre-opening to loosen their structure, followed by degumming to eliminate natural residues, resulting in cleaner fibers ready for spinning.

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.53-1.png)

### Final Preparation

In the final stage, we fine open the fibers to achieve optimal texture and consistency. Once they meet our quality standards, they are packaged for use as high-quality, ready-to-spin fibers.

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.54-1.png)

### Initial Processing

After harvesting, the fibers are transported to our facility, where we begin by brushing (optional) them to remove impurities. We then cut the fibers to the desired length for further refinement.

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.55-1.png)

### Refinement

Next, the fibers undergo pre-opening to loosen their structure, followed by degumming to eliminate natural residues, resulting in cleaner fibers ready for spinning.

![Nextevo Vietnam What We Do Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/10/WhatsApp-Image-2024-09-25-at-18.43.53-1.png)

### Final Preparation

In the final stage, we fine open the fibers to achieve optimal texture and consistency. Once they meet our quality standards, they are packaged for use as high-quality, ready-to-spin fibers.

Be a part of the sustainable fashion movement! Find out how Pineapple Leaf Fibre is paving the way for innovative and eco-friendly textiles.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/quotes/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/quotes/name/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/careers/contact-centre-officer-executive/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/careers/analysis-executive/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/category/uncategorized/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/1/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/23/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/20/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/16/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/13/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/12/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/9/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion

Embark on an eco-conscious journey, blending farm-to-fashion aesthetics, sustainability, and elegance to create inspiring moments.

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

## CIRCULARITY    & ZERO   WASTE

Where sustainability fuels fashion, making bold statements with every step towards an eco-chic future.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/iStock-1686472554-1.png)

A vital agricultural hub, Southeast Asia generates around 700-800 million tonnes of these byproducts annually, often left to rot or burnt, leading to methane pollution and crop diseases.

Nextevo repurpose these eco-friendly materials, replacing resource-intensive textiles like conventional cotton.

The heart of our philosophy lies in circularity and zero waste.

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/thorny-pineapples-closeup-market-Edited-1.png)

Over

### 10,000++ Mt

Of Agricultural Byproducts Generated Annually Globally

![Zero Waste Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/01/ekaterina-grosheva-wgRD0Lfe43Q-unsplash-1.png)

### 7.3 Mt

Of Alternative Natural Fibers To Cotton Fibers Produced Globally

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-3.png)

The pineapple’s global appeal and association with good fortune enhance the allure of our product marketing. We leverage the eco-friendly potential of pineapple leaves, a byproduct that requires no extra resources like land, water, or fertilizer, embodying sustainability in our products.

Our scalable sourcing methods ensure a reliable supply of pineapple leaves, backed by **FibreTrace®** technology, which tracks materials from farm to fiber for enhanced transparency and authenticity in our supply chain.

![Pineapple Image](https://nextevo.one/wp-content/uploads/2024/07/Untitled-design-4.png)

## Our Pineapple Leaf Fibre (PALF)

Our sustainable natural fiber textiles, which are OEKO-TEX® STANDARD 100 certified, are crafted from Pineapple Leaf Fibers (PALF) and offer characteristics similar to cotton. This makes them ideal for fashion apparel, accessories, footwear, home textiles, upholstery, and interior furnishings.

[Find Out More](https://nextevo.one/features/6/products/pineapple-leaf-fibre-palf/)

# Threads of Change:   Redefining Fashion
